  - 13
  concepts:

  stage: changed_example
  narration: 6.1.changed
  pdf:
  - lec06-lan.pdf
  - 14
  concepts:

  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - tex:
      name: f
      zone: top

      size: 40
  - text:
      name: tw
      zone: bottom
      lines:
      - 'Now: d = 20 μs at the same 10 Mb/s'
      when: Now change
  adjust: {}
- id: 6.1.predict
  stage: prediction
  narration: 6.1.predict
  pdf:
  - lec06-lan.pdf
  - 14
  concepts:
  - Collision Detection Window
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - pause:
      text: the minimum L in bits for d = 20 μs?
      long: true
  adjust: {}
  hold: 12
- id: 6.1.answer
  stage: explanation
  narration: 6.1.answer
  pdf:
  - lec06-lan.pdf
  - 14
  concepts:
  - Collision Detection Window
  - CSMA-CD
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - text:
      name: tw
      zone: bottom
      lines:
      - L ≥ 2 × 20 μs × 10 Mb/s = 400 bits
      - The formula's answer, not a new Ethernet minimum
  - rule:
      text: 'These limits belong to shared half-duplex wires. Next: stop sharing.'
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}

      right: Unknown unicast floods, but the destination address doesn't change
  adjust: {}

  stage: changed_example
  narration: 6.3.changed
  pdf:

      - - B to C

      when: Use this
  - net.walk:
      name: seven
      path:
      - S3
      - S2
      - S1
      - S7
      when: A to B
  adjust: {}
- id: 6.3.predict
  stage: prediction
  narration: 6.3.predict
  pdf:
  - lec06-lan.pdf
  - 41
  concepts:
  - Spanning Tree Protocol
  clarifications: []

  adjust: {}
  hold: 6
- id: 6.3.answer
  stage: explanation
  narration: 6.3.answer
  pdf:
  - lec06-lan.pdf
  - 41
  concepts:
  - Spanning Tree Protocol
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - net.walk:
      name: seven
      path:
      - S3
      - S2
      - S1
      - S4
      state: done
      when: A to C goes
  - table.set:
      name: paths
      r: 1
      c: 1
      v: S3 S2 S1 S4
      state: done
  - net.walk:
      name: seven
      path:
      - S7
      - S1
      - S4
      state: done
      when: B to C goes
  - table.set:
      name: paths
      r: 2
      c: 1
      v: S7 S1 S4
      state: done
  - net.walk:
      name: seven
      path:
      - S3
      - S2
      - S1
      - S7
      when: Notice that A
  - rule:
      text: A spanning tree promises no loops. It does not promise shortest paths.
      when: Spanning tree does
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}

        - D
        - s: control
      - - A
        - B
      - - B
        - D
  - packet.spawn:
      name: dp

      - ip

  - packet.hop:

  - rule:

  stage: rule
  narration: 7.1.pitfall
  pdf:

  - 13
  concepts:

  pdf:
  - lec07-network1.pdf
  - 15
  concepts:
  - Best-Effort Service
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - bullets:
      name: why
      zone: bottom
      show: 3
      items:
      - A full queue drops it
      - Its TTL expires
      - A failure or policy stops it
  - rule:
      text: 'No promise was broken. Next: addresses that scale.'
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}

- id: 8.2.changed
  stage: changed_example
  narration: 8.2.changed
  pdf:

  - 29
  concepts:

- id: 8.2.predict

  - Longest Prefix Matching

  adjust: {}
  hold: 12
- id: 8.2.answer
  stage: explanation
  narration: 8.2.answer
  pdf:
  - lec08-network2.pdf
  - 29
  concepts:
  - Longest Prefix Matching
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - table.mark:
      name: rt2
      r: 2
      state: done
      when: The slash 28 covers
  - bullets:
      name: an
      zone: bottom
      show: 0
      items:
      - 201.10.6.17 → /28 → D
      - 201.10.6.33 → /23 → B
  - reveal:
      name: an
      when: so 201.10.6.17
  - reveal:
      name: an
      when: And 201.10.6.33
  - table.mark:
      name: rt2
      r: 1
      state: done
  - clear:
      when: Finding the output
  - q:
      text: The packet still has to physically cross the router.
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}

      - - L

  - packet.hop:
      name: q

  - packet.flood:

  - packet.spawn:

  - seq.show:

      kind: transport

  - seq.msg:
      name: dn

      b: Root

      kind: ip
      when: The resolver then
  - seq.msg:
      name: dn
      a: Root
      b: Resolver
      text: try .test
      kind: data
  - seq.msg:
      name: dn
      a: Resolver
      b: .test
      text: query
      kind: ip

  - seq.msg:
      name: dn
      a: .test
      b: Resolver
      text: try example.test
      kind: data
  - seq.msg:
      name: dn
      a: Resolver
      b: example.test
      text: query
      kind: ip
      when: It asks that
  - seq.msg:
      name: dn
      a: example.test
      b: Resolver

      kind: data
      state: done
  - seq.msg:
      name: dn
      a: Resolver
      b: Laptop
      text: 203.0.113.80
      kind: transport
      state: done
      when: The result returns

  stage: rule
  narration: 9.2.rule-rec

  - lec09-discovery.pdf
  - 36
  concepts:
  - Recursive DNS Queries
  - Iterative DNS Queries
  clarifications: []

- id: 9.2.cache
  stage: state
  narration: 9.2.cache
  pdf:
  - lec09-discovery.pdf
  - 37
  concepts:
  - DNS Caching

  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - tl.show:
      name: cl

      tracks:
      - Authoritative
      - Cache
      span:
      - 0
      - 5
      ticks:
      - 0
      - 1
      - 2
      - 3
      - 4
      - 5
  - tl.block:
      name: cl
      track: Cache
      start: 0
      end: 5
      text: old address, TTL 300 s
      kind: ip
      when: Store a record
  - tl.block:
      name: cl
      track: Authoritative
      start: 0
      end: 1
      text: old
      kind: data
      when: If the real
  - tl.block:
      name: cl
      track: Authoritative
      start: 1
      end: 5
      text: new address
      state: done
  - rule:
      text: 'Long TTL: less load, staler answers. Short TTL: fresher answers, more
        load.'
  adjust: {}

  stage: state

  - lec09-discovery.pdf

  - lec09-discovery.pdf
  - 38

  - Negative DNS Caching
  clarifications: []

      - F

  - graph.promote:

      via: B
  - rule:

      when:

      name: bigt
      when: Check any of these
  - rule:

      when: Keep your settled

  stage: rule
  narration: 10.1.pitfall
  pdf:

  - 36
  concepts:

  stage: changed_example
  narration: 10.1.changed
  pdf:
  - lec10-routing.pdf
  - 36

      name: g
      a: B
      b: C
      cost: 6
  - table.show:
      name: chg
      zone: right_top
      cols:
      - Dest
      - Distance
      - First hop
      rows:
      - - B
        - '?'
        - '?'
      - - C
        - '?'
        - '?'
      - - D
        - '?'
        - '?'
  adjust: {}
- id: 10.1.predict
  stage: prediction
  narration: 10.1.predict
  pdf:
  - lec10-routing.pdf
  - 36
  concepts:

      text: the new distances, and A's first hop toward C?
      long: true

  hold: 12
- id: 10.1.answer
  stage: explanation
  narration: 10.1.answer
  pdf:
  - lec10-routing.pdf
  - 36
  concepts:
  - Dijkstra Algorithm
  - Link-State Routing
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - table.set:
      name: chg
      r: 0
      c: 1
      v: $routing.changed_dist.B
      when: Settle B at 2
  - net.pulse:
      name: g
      path:
      - B
      - C
      state: error
      when: Through B, C
  - rule:
      text: 'Through B, C would cost 8: it doesn''t beat 5'
      when:
        phrase: Through B, C
        frame_offset: 29
  - table.set:
      name: chg
      r: 2
      c: 1
      v: $routing.changed_dist.D
      when: D becomes 6
  - table.set:
      name: chg
      r: 1
      c: 1
      v: $routing.changed_dist.C
      state: now
      when: Then settle C
  - rule:
      text: 'Settle C at 5. Through C, D would cost 7: no change'
      when:
        phrase: Then settle C
        frame_offset: 20
  - net.pulse:
      name: g
      path:
      - C
      - D
      state: error
      when: Through C, D would cost 7
  - rule:
      text: 'Distances: 2, 5, 6'
      when: So the distances
  - table.set:
      name: chg
      r: 0
      c: 2
      v: $routing.changed_first_hop.B
      when: A's first hop to B
  - rule:
      text: 'First hops: B, C, B'
      when:
        phrase: A's first hop
        frame_offset: 20
  - table.set:
      name: chg
      r: 1
      c: 2
      v: $routing.changed_first_hop.C
      state: now
      when: to C is C
  - table.set:
      name: chg
      r: 2
      c: 2
      v: $routing.changed_first_hop.D
      when: and to D
  - net.walk:
      name: g
      path:
      - A
      - C
      when: One number changed
  - rule:
      text: One number changed A's route to C
      when:
        phrase: One number changed
        frame_offset: 32
  - net.pulse:
      name: g
      path:
      - B
      - C
      state: error
      when: the new cost had to reach it
  - rule:
      text: 'Next: what if routers share distances instead of a map?'
      when: Flooding carries
  adjust: {}

dish: '10.3'
title: Path vector
deck: lec10-routing.pdf
target_min: 3.5
concepts:
- Path-Vector Routing
pad: 2.2
beats:
- id: 10.3.question
  stage: question
  narration: 10.3.question
  pdf:
  - lec10-routing.pdf
  - 47
  concepts:
  - Path-Vector Routing
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - heading:
      text: 'Lecture 10: path vector'
  - q:
      text: Would a network share its map, or trust a rival's number?
  adjust: {}
- id: 10.3.limits
  stage: animation
  narration: 10.3.limits
  pdf:
  - lec10-routing.pdf
  - 48
  concepts:
  - Path-Vector Routing
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - table.show:
      name: lim
      zone: top
      cols:
      - Method
      - Problem between organizations
      rows:
      - - Link state
        - reveals topology; needs a shared metric
      - - Distance vector
        - one number hides the path
  - rule:
      text: Link state reveals topology and needs a common metric
      when: Link state floods
  - rule:
      text: Distance vector hides the structure
      when: Distance vector hides
  - rule:
      text: Costs aren't comparable across networks
      when: Different networks
  - rule:
      text: The number hides the route, so no preferences
      when: And the number hides
  adjust: {}
- id: 10.3.fix
  stage: state
  narration: 10.3.fix
  pdf:
  - lec10-routing.pdf
  - 50
  concepts:
  - Path-Vector Routing
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - net.show:
      name: pv
      zone: bottom
      nodes:
        n1:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 0
          - 0
          l: AS 1
        n2:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 3.5
          - 0
          l: AS 2
        n3:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 7
          - 0
          l: AS 3
        n4:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 10.5
          - 0
          l: AS 4
      links:
      - - n1
        - n2
      - - n2
        - n3
      - - n3
        - n4
  - rule:
      text: 'The fix: advertise the whole path'
      when:
        phrase: The fix is
        frame_offset: 41
  - msg.send:
      name: pv
      a: n1
      b: n2

  adjust: {}

dish: '11.3'
title: BGP that won't settle
deck: lec11-convergence.pdf
target_min: 7.5
concepts:
- BGP Session Liveness
- BGP Path Exploration
- Stable Paths Problem
- Policy Oscillation
pad: 2.2
beats:

  stage: question

  - tl.block:

      end: 66
      kind: ip
  - tl.block:

      end: 246
      text: 'silent: 180 s hold timer'

        peer is invalid.'

  - 28

  - bullets:

      zone: top
      show: 3

- id: 12.3.nexthop

  - table.reveal:

- id: 12.3.order
  stage: rule
  narration: 12.3.order
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 24
  concepts:
  - Local Preference
  - Hot-Potato Routing
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - stack.show:
      name: order
      zone: left
      width: 6
      layers:
      - t: 1. Highest local preference
        k: ip
      - t: 2. Shortest AS path
        k: eth
      - t: 3. Lowest internal exit cost
        k: transport
      - t: 4. Tie break
        k: data
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 0
      when: Highest local
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 0
      state: null
      when: Then shortest
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 1
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 1
      state: null
      when: Then lowest
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 2
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 2
      state: null
      when: Then a tie
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 3
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 3
      state: null
      when: Compare in order
  - text:
      name: nosum
      zone: right
      lines:
      - Compare in order
      - Stop when one route remains
      - Never added into a score
      when: These are not
  adjust: {}
- id: 12.3.decide
  stage: state
  narration: 12.3.decide
  pdf:

  - 26
  concepts:
  - Local Preference
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - fade:
      names: nosum
  - sieve.show:
      name: cand
      zone: right
      rungs:
      - Local pref
      - AS length
      - Exit cost

      - name: A
        vals:
        - 140
        - 4
        - 50
      - name: B
        vals:
        - 100
        - 2
        - 5
      - name: C
        vals:
        - 100
        - 2
        - 1
      when: Try three
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 0
      when: With all three
  - sieve.step:
      name: cand
      rung: 0
      keep:
      - A
  - sieve.win:
      name: cand
      route: A
      unused:
      - 1
      - 2
      when: despite being
  - sieve.show:
      name: cand
      zone: right
      rungs:
      - Local pref
      - AS length
      - Exit cost
      routes:
      - name: B
        vals:
        - 100
        - 2
        - 5
      - name: C
        vals:
        - 100
        - 2
        - 1
      when: Withdraw A
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 0
      state: null
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 2
      when: so internal cost
  - sieve.step:
      name: cand
      rung: 2
      keep:
      - C
  - sieve.win:
      name: cand
      route: C
      when: C wins
  - sieve.show:
      name: cand
      zone: right
      rungs:
      - Local pref
      - AS length
      - Exit cost
      routes:
      - name: B
        vals:
        - 100
        - 1
        - 5
      - name: C
        vals:
        - 100
        - 2
        - 1
      when: Now shorten
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 2
      state: null
  - stack.mark:
      name: order
      layer: 1
      when: B beats C
  - sieve.step:
      name: cand
      rung: 1
      keep:
      - B
  - sieve.win:
      name: cand
      route: B
      unused:
      - 2
      when: and its higher
  adjust: {}
  pre_roll: 0.6
- id: 12.3.hot
  stage: state
  narration: 12.3.hot
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 25
  concepts:
  - Hot-Potato Routing
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - net.show:
      name: h
      zone: top
      nodes:
        W:
          k: box
          at:
          - 0
          - 0
          l: West exit
        R:
          k: router
          at:
          - 3.6
          - 1.4
          l: R
        S:
          k: router
          at:
          - 7.4
          - -1.4
          l: S
        E:
          k: box
          at:
          - 11
          - 0
          l: East exit
      links:
      - - R
        - W
        - c: 4
      - - R
        - E
        - c: 9
          s: dim
      - - S
        - W
        - c: 12
          s: dim
      - - S
        - E
        - c: 3
  - net.pulse:
      name: h
      path:
      - R
      - W
      when: A router with
  - net.path:
      name: h
      path:
      - R
      - W
      style: tree
      when: picks west
  - net.pulse:
      name: h
      path:
      - S
      - E
      when: A router on the other
  - net.path:
      name: h
      path:
      - S
      - E
      style: tree
      when: picks east
  - rule:
      text: 'Hot potato: when earlier attributes tie, use the nearest exit. Paths
        may be asymmetric.'
      when: Internet paths
  adjust: {}

  adjust: {}
---
dish: '12.4'
title: Money shapes paths
deck: lec12-bgp.pdf
target_min: 7
concepts:
- Customer-Provider Relationships
- Peering
- Gao-Rexford Conditions
- Valley-Free Paths
pad: 2.2
beats:
- id: 12.4.question
  stage: question
  narration: 12.4.question
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 34
  concepts:
  - Customer-Provider Relationships
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - heading:
      text: 'Lecture 12: money shapes paths'
  - q:
      text: If you ran a network, whose traffic would you carry for free?
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}
- id: 12.4.rel
  stage: animation
  narration: 12.4.rel
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 35
  concepts:
  - Customer-Provider Relationships
  - Peering
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - net.show:
      name: r
      zone: top
      nodes:
        P:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 0
          - 1.5
          l: Provider
        Q:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 6
          - 1.5
          l: Peer
        C:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 0
          - 0
          l: Customer
      links:
      - - C
        - P
        - s: up
      - - P
        - Q
        - c: peer
  - msg.send:
      name: r
      a: C
      b: P
      text: pays
      when: the customer pays
  - net.state:
      name: r
      nodes:
        C: now
      when: We draw
  - msg.send:
      name: r
      a: P
      b: Q
      text: own traffic
      when: In peering
  - net.state:
      name: r
      nodes:
        C: null
        Q: now
      when: A peer isn't
  - rule:
      text: Customer pays provider. Peers exchange only their own and their customers'
        traffic.
  adjust: {}
- id: 12.4.export
  stage: rule
  narration: 12.4.export
  pdf:

  - rule:

  - table.reveal:

      n: 2
      when: If a route came
  - table.reveal:
      name: ex
      n: 2
      when: If it came
  - table.mark:
      name: ex
      r: 2
      state: now
      when: Telling a provider
  adjust: {}

  - trap:
      wrong: A physically connected path is always usable
      right: Export policy can forbid a short, connected route

  stage: changed_example
  narration: 12.4.changed
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 41
  concepts:

  - net.show:

          at:

          - 2

        A:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 2
          - 1
          l: AS A
        P:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 5
          - 1
          l: Peer P
        C:
          k: cloud
          at:
          - 2
          - 0
          l: Customer C
      links:
      - - A
        - U
        - s: up
      - - A
        - P
      - - C
        - A
        - s: up
  - msg.send:
      name: a
      a: P
      b: A
      text: route

  - msg.send:
      name: a
      a: C
      b: A
      text: route
      when: And it learns
  adjust: {}
- id: 12.4.predict
  stage: prediction
  narration: 12.4.predict
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 41
  concepts:
  - BGP Export Policy
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - pause:
      text: who hears P's route? who hears C's route?
  adjust: {}
  hold: 7
- id: 12.4.answer
  stage: explanation
  narration: 12.4.answer
  pdf:
  - lec12-bgp.pdf
  - 41
  concepts:
  - BGP Export Policy
  - Customer-Provider Relationships
  - Peering
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - table.show:
      name: an
      zone: right
      cols:
      - Route from
      - Told to
      rows:
      - - Peer P
        - customer C only
      - - Customer C
        - provider U and peer P
      show: 0
  - msg.send:
      name: a
      a: A
      b: C
      text: P's route
      when: P's route goes
  - table.reveal:
      name: an
  - net.state:
      name: a
      links:
      - - A
        - U
        - blocked
      when: Telling U
  - net.state:
      name: a
      links:
      - - A
        - U
        - up
      when: C's route goes
  - msg.send:
      name: a
      a: A
      b: U
      text: C's route
      t: 1.1
  - msg.send:
      name: a
      a: A
      b: P
      text: C's route
      t: 1.1
  - table.reveal:
      name: an
  - clear:
      when: So far, every
  - q:
      text: What if one address means several servers, or a thousand receivers want
        one stream?
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}
  pre_roll: 1.0

dish: '13.1'
title: One address, many servers
deck: lec13-multicast.pdf
target_min: 4.5
concepts:
- IP Anycast

  - q:

      zone: bottom
  adjust: {}

  - chips.reveal:
      name: c
      when: Sender one uses
  - chips.reveal:
      name: c
      when: Sender two uses
  - tex:
      name: d
      zone: bottom
      tex: c_1\cdot c_2 = 1-1-1+1 = 0
      size: 48
      when: Multiply them
  adjust: {}
- id: 14.2.send
  stage: state
  narration: 14.2.send
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 15
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []
  moves:

  - chips.show:
      name: w
      zone: full
      rows:
      - label: c1, bit +1
        values: $wireless.first_rows.a
      - label: c2, bit -1
        values: $wireless.c2
      - label: z
        values: $wireless.first.z
      show: 0
  - chips.reveal:
      name: w
      when: Sender one sends
  - chips.reveal:
      name: w
      when: Sender two sends
  - chips.flip:
      name: w
      row: 1
      values: $wireless.first_rows.b
      label: c2, bit -1
      when: so it transmits its code flipped
  - chips.sum:
      name: w
      when: On the air
  - rule:
      text: The sum on the air looks like neither sender.
      when: This sum
  adjust: {}
- id: 14.2.decode
  stage: state
  narration: 14.2.decode
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 16
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - chips.show:
      name: w
      zone: left
      rows:
      - label: z
        values: $wireless.first.z
      - label: c1
        values: $wireless.c1
      - label: c2
        values: $wireless.c2
  - chips.dot:
      name: w
      a: 0
      b: 1
      products: $wireless.first_rows.corr1
      total: 4
      result: '+1'
      when: 0 times 1
      t: 4.5
  - chips.dot:
      name: w
      a: 0
      b: 2
      products: $wireless.first_rows.corr2
      total: -4
      result: '-1'
      when: With c 2
      t: 4
  - rule:
      text: Correlating with c1 multiplies sender two by c1 · c2, and that's zero.
      when: Why does this
  adjust: {}
- id: 14.2.rule
  stage: rule
  narration: 14.2.rule
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 16
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - tex:
      name: f1
      zone: top
      tex: z=\sum_k d_k\,c_k
      size: 54
      when: In formula form
  - tex:
      name: f2
      zone: bottom
      tex: d_k=\frac{z\cdot c_k}{M}
      size: 54
      when: The receiver takes
  - rule:
      text: 'Ideal model: synchronized chips, balanced power.'
      when: This is an ideal
  adjust: {}
  pre_roll: 0.7
- id: 14.2.pitfall
  stage: rule
  narration: 14.2.pitfall
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 16
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []

  - trap:

- id: 14.2.changed
  stage: changed_example
  narration: 14.2.changed
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 16
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - chips.show:
      name: w
      zone: upper
      rows:
      - label: c1, bit -1
        values: $wireless.second_rows.a
      - label: c2, bit -1
        values: $wireless.second_rows.b
      - label: z
        values: $wireless.second.z
      show: 2
  adjust: {}
- id: 14.2.predict
  stage: prediction
  narration: 14.2.predict
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 16
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - pause:
      text: the new z? what does each sender decode to?
      long: true
  adjust: {}
  hold: 12
- id: 14.2.answer
  stage: explanation
  narration: 14.2.answer
  pdf:
  - lec14-wireless.pdf
  - 16
  concepts:
  - CDMA
  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - chips.sum:
      name: w
      when: z is minus 2
  - tex:
      name: dc1
      zone: lower
      tex: z\cdot c_1/4=(-2+0+0-2)/4=-1
      size: 38
      when: Correlate with c 1
  - tex:
      name: dc2
      zone: lower
      tex: z\cdot c_2/4=(-2+0+0-2)/4=-1
      size: 38
      when: Correlate with c 2
  - rule:
      text: 'Both decode to −1. Next: joining a Wi-Fi network.'
      when: Both bits
  - keep: {}
  adjust: {}

  - bullets:
      name: na
      zone: top
      show: 0
      items:
      - Noise may have corrupted the data
      - The ACK itself may have been lost
      - A link-layer ACK repairs one radio hop, not TCP's job
  - reveal:
      name: na
      when: The data might
  - reveal:
      name: na
      when: or the ACK
  - reveal:
      name: na
      when: Note too
  - rule:
      text: No ACK does not prove a collision. Local recovery repairs one hop, not
        the whole path.
      when: Local repair
  adjust: {}

- id: 14.4.tradeoff

  - 26
  concepts:

  clarifications: []
  moves:
  - clear: {}
  - bullets:
      name: tr
      zone: top
      show: 0
      items:
      - An RTS collision is short, so it costs little
      - The four-frame exchange adds overhead
      - Short frames on a quiet channel may lose more than they save
  - reveal:
      name: tr
      when: RTS frames can
  - reveal:
      name: tr
      when: And the four-frame
  - reveal:
      name: tr
      when: For short frames
  - rule:
      text: RTS/CTS trades overhead for cheaper collisions. Good for long frames under
        contention.
      when: So choose
  adjust: {}

---
dish: '15.1'
title: The cellular core
deck: lec15-mobile.pdf
target_min: 6.5
concepts:
- 2G Circuit-Switched Core
- 3G Parallel Voice and Data
- LTE All-IP Core
- UE and Subscriber Identity
- LTE eNodeB
- LTE Home Subscriber Server
- LTE Mobility Management Entity
- LTE Serving Gateway
- LTE Packet Gateway
pad: 2.2
beats:
